# Slice 11 RED to GREEN record (P2-S11-AC-044)

Date: 2026-10-12. Criterion: "Execute Contract → QA-RED → data, API, SSR and island implementation → QA-GREEN → refactor; retain failing-test evidence and run canonical validation."

This file retains the failing-test evidence of Slice 11: for each work package, the contract that was written first, the failing test (RED) with the failure that was observed, the fix and the passing result (GREEN), and any refactor. It is guarded by `tests/contracts/phase-02-slice-11-process-record.test.ts`.

## Sources and limits

- Compiled ONLY from the lane reports named below. No entry was added from memory, from commit history or from a re-run. The Claude-session reports (work from `618ec5c1`, 2026-10-10 to 2026-10-12) are in `docs/handoffs/2026-10-10-claude-s11/`; the Codex-session reports (the earlier work, 2026-10-08 to 2026-10-10) are in `docs/handoffs/2026-10-08-phase2-codex-handoff/s11/`. Both directories are the retained copies; the raw TAP, vitest and Playwright logs they cite lived in the git-ignored `.lane-logs/` and `lane-*-logs/` directories of the lane worktrees and are not part of this record.
- Claude-session lane reports: `lane-a11y-status-report.md`, `lane-assign-report.md`, `lane-bench-report.md`, `lane-bench2-report.md`, `lane-bundle-report.md`, `lane-checker-report.md`, `lane-dep502-report.md`, `lane-e2e-report.md`, `lane-evidence-gaps-report.md`, `lane-fe-report.md`, `lane-finisher-report.md`, `lane-gates-report.md`, `lane-integration-report.md`, `lane-last-gaps-report.md`, `lane-lease-report.md`, `lane-misc-report.md`, `lane-order-report.md`, `lane-preflight-cursor-report.md`, `lane-preview-projection-report.md`, `lane-publication-report.md`, `lane-queue-perf-report.md`, `lane-recheck-report.md`, `lane-reviewA-2-report.md`, `lane-reviewA1-report.md`, `lane-reviewB-1-report.md`, `lane-reviewB-2-report.md`, `lane-sql-small-report.md`, `lane-worker-report.md`.
- Codex-session lane reports: `lane-s11-0-report.md`, `lane-s11-1-report.md`, `lane-s11-1r-report.md`, `lane-s11-2-report.md`, `lane-s11-3a-report.md`, `lane-s11-3b-report.md`, `lane-s11-3c-report.md`, `lane-s11-3d-report.md`, `lane-s11-3s-report.md`, `lane-s11-4-report.md`, `lane-s11-4a-report.md`, `lane-s11-4r-report.md`, `lane-s11-5-report.md`, `lane-s11-g-report.md`, `lane-s11-sr-report.md`.
- The order of work (contract, RED, GREEN, refactor) is what each lane reported. No test can prove the chronology. What the guard proves is that every record entry is complete, that every contract file it names exists, and that every RED identity named here still exists in the repository.
- Identity convention: `RED: <file> :: <identity>` names a test title that is a whole string literal in that file (pgTAP description, Vitest title or Playwright title). `RED (fragment)` is used for runner assertions whose title is built from a template: the identity is a fixed part of the title. A title a later lane renamed is named by its current wording; the earlier wording stays in the observed text.
- Where a lane report gave TAP positions (`not ok 14`) or a range instead of titles, the identity is the assertion in the current file that carries the behaviour the report describes, and the lane's own wording stays in the observed text, as in the Slice 10 record. Per-package counts (for example "47/47") are the lane's own reported result at the time.
- A lane item with no observed failure is not a work package. It is listed under Characterization and spec-only work: green on arrival, evidence written after the code, mutation-only proof, spec-only work and updates to existing tests.
- Package IDs are one letter and two digits. The letter names the theme of the lane that reported the work, the `Source` line names the exact report: A, C, F, L, P, R, S, V (database and Worker lanes of the first Claude wave), G, H, J, K, D, Q (review-fix lanes, response contract, reviewer-queue plan), E, M, N, T, X, U, I (web, checker, bundle and integration lanes), B, O, Y (benchmark, ordering and gate lanes), Z51 and above (the Codex session).

## Inconsistencies between lane reports

Noted while compiling this record. None is resolved here; each is the compiler's reading of two statements that do not agree, and the current repository text wins wherever a title or file is named.

- reviewA1 and reviewB-1 both claim `import-graph.test-support.ts`, `import-graph.test.ts` and `cms-editorial-principal-boundaries.test-support.ts` as new files of their own lane.
- The sweep job deadline is claimed by reviewA-2, by reviewB-2 and by commit `b001ff2d`. The two failing deadline cases are RED in reviewA-2 and a baseline in reviewB-2. The record carries it once, in H02.
- The worker lane reported that the sweep needed no change and tested a per-call 15,000 ms window; reviewA-2 later replaced that with one shared job deadline.
- The reviewA1 counts for `rls_session_scope` (57 assertions) and the titles of `schema_posture` and `evidence_audit` predate the relationship policies of `20261010170200`; lane queue-perf reports 68 of 68 for the same file.
- reviewA1 left open that relationship scopes cannot be row predicates; the finisher lane later added them (DEC-174).
- The worker lane's item 1 awaits a 422-versus-502 ruling; lane dep502 cites the owner ratification of 2026-10-11.
- BE00:172 lists 504 under `DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE`; lane dep502 left 504 as `GATEWAY_TIMEOUT`.
- Lane publication: its "Verification status" section is empty; its RED TAP also shows a failing control (`seventeen due schedules`, have 18) and a missing function (`cms_lineage_head_observation`) that the report never quotes; its own list says "twelve" to "eighteen".
- Lane finisher's "18 of 32 fail" is the prove count; the TAP has 14 `not ok` lines in that file because tests 24-32 ran beyond a stale plan of 23.
- Lane assign reports 21 `ok -` lines for runner 010 and 23 assertions for `assign_refusals`; the finisher reports 22 `ok -` lines and the current file plans 25.
- Lane lease: new files have 6 and 6 tests at RED and 7 and 7 at GREEN, unexplained.
- Lane recheck: the RED TAP uses `cms_review_dependency_*` function names, the report and final code `cms_dependency_recheck_*`.
- Lane sql-small: the AC-119 file was restructured and not re-run against the unfixed guard; its report was persisted by the orchestrator after the lane's own write was refused.
- BE03b line numbers cited by several reports (1721, 1773, 1793, 1805, 1809, 1846, 1861-65, 1898, 2012-16) no longer match the current specification text; they are kept as cited, in plain text.
- Real-route Playwright totals differ: the e2e lane reports 148 tests, the bundle lane 151 of 151 (it added one test). The bundle lane also reports one unfixed RR-07 flake that the e2e lane does not see.
- Max-input timing: the integration lane measures the Worker at the node maximum at 321 to 483 ms; the e2e lane re-measured the same gate at 76 to 100 ms. `ACCESSIBILITY_NODES_MAX` is 256 in the checker lane's open items and 12,288 in the integration lane's.
- FE03:1097 is described by two lanes as a blanket author exclusion that conflicts with BE03b; the current text reads "for action publish only".
- Lane bench: it says it waited for load below 1.5 before each run, then that the load was gated only before run 1.
- The canonical gate `claude-dbverify-20261011-035212` failed the bench assertion (p95 309.5 ms); its commit is not given. The bench lane reports `phase_02_slice_11_recheck_run.sql` test 47 as flaky; `STATE.md` later lists a rank-aware recheck test at `813fcafd` without linking the two.
- `STATE.md` titles the 2026-10-11 ~14:55Z update "Update 2026-11".
- No assigned report or `STATE.md` states a result for the diagnostic `pnpm validate`; the evidence runner (`scripts/evidence/run-slice-evidence.mjs`) is reported as not run by the evidence-gaps and last-gaps lanes.
- Codex session, E2 fixture: S11-3d's final run says all seven e2 files are green; S11-SR (five files) and S11-G (seven files, two of them Slice 10 keyset files) later say they abort with `CONFLICT` because of S11-SR's stricter review CAS guard, and S11-G records the fixture fix as not done.
- Codex session, executor rule: S11-3b and S11-SR say the executor never cancels (DEC-158(d)) and S11-SR calls the BE03b line "cancelled with entry_unavailable" contradictory; the current `execute_refusals` title says the executor cancels for an archived entry (DEC-173 supersedes DEC-158(d) for that case). The current titles are used.
- Codex session: S11-3a reports 15 assertions for race runner 011 in R4 and 18 `ok` in its verification section, and RED "13/24" against GREEN "25/25" for the submit refusals file. S11-2 names migration 017000 `cms_revision_state_constant.sql`; the file today is `cms_revision_entry_key.sql`.
- Codex session: S11-4r and S11-5 both blame line 187 of migration 017560 for a `delete` without `WHERE`; no report in the set records the fix. S11-4r defects 1 and 2 and S11-5 CP-F, and S11-G CP8 (seven aborting e2 and keyset files, the bench128 p95), were observed and left unfixed in those reports.
- The two-digit package id limits the Codex session to 49 ids (Z51 to Z99): related items of one lane (the S11-3s helpers, the S11-4a checker modules, the S11-1R remediation) share a package with several RED lines.

## Canonical validation

Runs reported by the lane reports and by the orchestrator state file, copied faithfully. A run is called canonical only where its source calls it a canonical (quiet-host) gate; every other run is a lane run or a diagnostic and says so.
